feat(runtime): improve error handling for worker progress callbacks

- Reordered exceptions in the heartbeat loop for clearer differentiation of error types.
- Added missing `status_code` logging for `WorkerCallbackRejectedError`.
- Introduced a new test to validate logging behavior for rejected progress callbacks without traceback.

def test_validate_snapshot_missing_snapshot_key_422() -> None:
    """Test /validate-snapshot returns 422 if 'snapshot' key is missing."""
    req = {"verbosity": "info"}
    response = client.post("/validate-snapshot", json=req)
    assert response.status_code == 422
    data = response.json()
    assert "validationMessages" in data or "detail" in data

def test_validate_snapshot_with_warnings() -> None:
    """Test validation with warnings."""
    # Provide snapshot that triggers a warning (empty lists are valid, but may trigger
    # warnings)
    response = client.post(
        "/validate-snapshot",
        json={"snapshot": minimal_valid_snapshot(), "verbosity": "warning"},
    )
    assert response.status_code == 200
Attachment Name: solver_v1_api.py
Attachments Kind: Visible

Attachments Text:
@router.post(
    "/validate-snapshot",
    responses={
        200: {"model": ValidationResponse, "description": "Successful validation"},
        260: {"model": ValidationResponse, "description": "Business validation errors"},
        422: {
            "description": "Schema validation errors (FastAPI default format)",
            "content": {
                "application/json": {
                    "schema": {
                        "type": "object",
                        "properties": {"detail": {"type": "array", "items": {"type": "object"}}},
                    }
                }
            },
        },
        500: {"description": "Internal server error"},
    },
    tags=["SolverV1"],
    summary="Validate snapshot data without running the solver",
    response_model_by_alias=True,
)
async def validate_snapshot(
    request: SnapshotValidationRequest,
) -> Response:
    """
    Validate snapshot data without running the solver.

    Implements message collection and response logic as specified in SPEC_22.
    Always returns a ValidationResponse object, never ein JSONResponse.
    """
    try:
        logger.info("Received request to validate snapshot")
        logger.debug(
            "Validation snapshot summary: demands=%d, work_plans=%d, articles=%d, equipment=%d, "
            "equipment_unavailabilities=%d, worker_availability=%d, worker_availability_entries=%d, "
            "worker_qualifications=%d, packaging_compatibility=%d",
            *_snapshot_log_metrics(request.snapshot),
        )
        verbosity: str = request.verbosity if request.verbosity is not None else "info"
        messages, status_code = collect_validation_messages(request.snapshot, verbosity)
        response = ValidationResponse.model_validate({"validationMessages": messages})
        if status_code == VALIDATION_BUSINESS_ERROR_CODE:
            return Response(
                content=response.model_dump_json(by_alias=True),
                status_code=VALIDATION_BUSINESS_ERROR_CODE,
                media_type="application/json",
            )
        return Response(
            content=response.model_dump_json(by_alias=True),
            status_code=200,
            media_type="application/json",
        )
    except ValidationError as err:
        logger.exception("Schema validation error during snapshot validation")
        response = ValidationResponse.model_validate({"validationMessages": []})
        raise HTTPException(
            status_code=422,
            detail=response.model_dump(by_alias=True),
        ) from err
    except HTTPException:
        raise
    except Exception as exc:
        logger.exception("Unhandled exception during snapshot validation")
        raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail="Internal server error") from exc

kotlin.Unit

==== SimpleCompletableMessage #2 ====
Assistant: 
## Kurzfassung

Bei der Snapshot-Validierung gibt es **zwei Fehlerklassen**:

1. **Schema-/Request-Validierungsfehler → HTTP 422**  
   Der Request passt nicht zum erwarteten JSON/Pydantic-Schema, z. B. `snapshot` fehlt, falscher Typ, Pflichtfeld fehlt.

2. **Business-Validierungsfehler → HTTP 260**  
   Der Snapshot ist formal parsebar, verletzt aber fachliche Konsistenzregeln. Diese Fehler kommen als `validationMessages` mit `level: "error"` zurück.

Zusätzlich gibt es **Warnings** (`level: "warning"`), die **keinen Fehlerstatus** auslösen, solange keine Error-Meldung dabei ist. Dann bleibt der HTTP-Status **200**.

---

## 1. Schema-/Request-Validierungsfehler `422`

Diese entstehen, bevor die eigentliche Business-Validierung läuft.

### Beispiele

#### `snapshot` fehlt komplett

```json
{
  "verbosity": "info"
}
```

Ergebnis: **HTTP 422**

#### Falscher Typ für `snapshot`

```json
{
  "snapshot": "not-an-object",
  "verbosity": "info"
}
```

Ergebnis: **HTTP 422**

#### Falscher Typ in einem Feld

Beispiel: ein Feld erwartet eine Zahl, bekommt aber Text.

```json
{
  "snapshot": {
    "articles": [
      {
        "articleId": "A1",
        "relDensityMin": "abc"
      }
    ]
  }
}
```

Ergebnis: **HTTP 422**, sofern das Feld laut Schema numerisch ist.

#### Falscher Wert für `verbosity`

Wenn nur definierte Werte wie `debug`, `info`, `warning`, `error` erlaubt sind und etwas anderes kommt:

```json
{
  "snapshot": {},
  "verbosity": "verbose"
}
```

Ergebnis: typischerweise **HTTP 422** oder später ein interner Fehler, abhängig davon, wie strikt das Request-Modell validiert.

---

## 2. Business-Validierungsfehler `260`

Diese entstehen durch fachliche Prüfungen im Snapshot. Das sind die eigentlichen Snapshot-Validierungsfehler.

### 2.1 Doppelte oder leere IDs

Geprüft werden:

- `demands[].demandId`
- `articles[].articleId`
- `workPlans[].workPlanId`
- `equipment[].equipmentId`
- `workerAvailability[].worker.workerId`
- `workerQualifications[].worker.workerId`

#### Doppelte Demand-IDs

```json
{
  "snapshot": {
    "demands": [
      { "demandId": "D1", "articleId": "A1" },
      { "demandId": "D1", "articleId": "A2" }
    ]
  }
}
```

Meldung:

```text
Demand IDs must be unique. Duplicates found: D1.
```

#### Leere Article-ID

```json
{
  "snapshot": {
    "articles": [
      { "articleId": "", "workPlanId": "WP1" }
    ]
  }
}
```

Meldung:

```text
Article IDs must not be empty. Empty IDs found: .
```

---

### 2.2 Artikel referenziert nicht existierenden Arbeitsplan

Jeder `articles[].workPlanId` muss in `workPlans[].workPlanId` existieren.

#### Beispiel

```json
{
  "snapshot": {
    "articles": [
      {
        "articleId": "A1",
        "workPlanId": "WP_DOES_NOT_EXIST"
      }
    ],
    "workPlans": [
      {
        "workPlanId": "WP1"
      }
    ]
  }
}
```

Meldung:

```text
Missing work plans for the following IDs: WP_DOES_NOT_EXIST
```

---

### 2.3 Demand referenziert nicht existierenden Artikel

Jedes `demands[].articleId` muss in `articles[].articleId` existieren.

#### Beispiel

```json
{
  "snapshot": {
    "demands": [
      {
        "demandId": "D1",
        "articleId": "A_DOES_NOT_EXIST"
      }
    ],
    "articles": [
      {
        "articleId": "A1"
      }
    ]
  }
}
```

Meldung:

```text
Missing articles for the following demand article IDs: A_DOES_NOT_EXIST
```

---

### 2.4 Ungültige Dichte am Artikel

Für jeden Artikel muss `relDensityMin` vorhanden und **> 0** sein.

#### Beispiel: `relDensityMin` ist `0`

```json
{
  "snapshot": {
    "articles": [
      {
        "articleId": "A1",
        "relDensityMin": 0
      }
    ]
  }
}
```

Meldung:

```text
Article A1 has invalid rel_density_min: 0. Must be greater than 0.
```

#### Beispiel: `relDensityMin` fehlt bzw. ist `null`

```json
{
  "snapshot": {
    "articles": [
      {
        "articleId": "A1",
        "relDensityMin": null
      }
    ]
  }
}
```

Meldung:

```text
Article A1 has invalid rel_density_min: None. Must be greater than 0.
```

---

### 2.5 Artikel ohne Department / Disponent2

Jeder Artikel muss eine nicht-leere `departmentId` haben.

#### Beispiel

```json
{
  "snapshot": {
    "articles": [
      {
        "articleId": "A1",
        "articleName": "Artikel 1",
        "departmentId": ""
      }
    ]
  }
}
```

Meldung:

```text
Articles missing required department (Disponent2): A1 (Artikel 1)
```

---

### 2.6 Artikel-Department kommt in keinem Equipment vor

Alle `articles[].departmentId` müssen durch mindestens ein `equipment[].departments[]` abgedeckt sein, sofern Equipment-Departments überhaupt gepflegt sind.

#### Beispiel

```json
{
  "snapshot": {
    "articles": [
      {
        "articleId": "A1",
        "departmentId": "30100"
      }
    ],
    "equipment": [
      {
        "equipmentId": "E1",
        "equipmentKey": "EQ1",
        "departments": ["99999"]
      }
    ]
  }
}
```

Meldung:

```text
Departments (Disponent2) referenced by articles were not found: 30100
```

---

### 2.7 Fehlende `workItemConfigs` am Artikel

Alle Work-Item-Keys, die im zugehörigen Arbeitsplan vorkommen, müssen im Artikel unter `workItemConfigs` vorhanden sein.

#### Beispiel

Arbeitsplan verlangt `HE01`, Artikel hat aber kein `HE01`.

```json
{
  "snapshot": {
    "workPlans": [
      {
        "workPlanId": "WP1",
        "manufacturing": {
          "workItemKey": "HE01"
        }
      }
    ],
    "articles": [
      {
        "articleId": "A1",
        "workPlanId": "WP1",
        "workItemConfigs": []
      }
    ]
  }
}
```

Meldung:

```text
Article A1 is missing work_item_configs for: HE01. Cannot process article.
```

---

### 2.8 Start-/End-Operation fehlt

Jeder Artikel muss jeweils eine `workItemConfig` für:

- `HE01`
- `ABF01`

haben.

#### Beispiel: `HE01` fehlt

```json
{
  "snapshot": {
    "articles": [
      {
        "articleId": "A1",
        "workItemConfigs": [
          {
            "workItemKey": "ABF01",
            "netTimeFactor": 1,
            "rampUpTime": 0
          }
        ]
      }
    ]
  }
}
```

Meldung:

```text
Article A1 is missing work_item_config for HE01. Cannot process article.
```

#### Beispiel: `ABF01` fehlt

```json
{
  "snapshot": {
    "articles": [
      {
        "articleId": "A1",
        "workItemConfigs": [
          {
            "workItemKey": "HE01",
            "netTimeFactor": 1,
            "rampUpTime": 0
          }
        ]
      }
    ]
  }
}
```

Meldung:

```text
Article A1 is missing work_item_config for ABF01. Cannot process article.
```

---

### 2.9 Start-/End-Operation hat keine positive Zeit

Für `HE01` und `ABF01` muss mindestens eines der Felder positiv sein:

- `netTimeFactor > 0`
- oder `rampUpTime > 0`

#### Beispiel

```json
{
  "snapshot": {
    "articles": [
      {
        "articleId": "A1",
        "workItemConfigs": [
          {
            "workItemKey": "HE01",
            "netTimeFactor": 0,
            "rampUpTime": 0
          },
          {
            "workItemKey": "ABF01",
            "netTimeFactor": 1,
            "rampUpTime": 0
          }
        ]
      }
    ]
  }
}
```

Meldung:

```text
Article A1 has HE01 work_item_config with net_time_factor=0, ramp_up_time=0. At least one of net_time_factor or ramp_up_time must be > 0. This prevents a valid processing chain.
```

---

### 2.10 Fehlendes Equipment für benötigten Work-Item-Key

Für jeden benötigten Work-Item-Key muss mindestens ein kompatibles Equipment existieren.

Ausgenommen sind aktuell:

- `QS01`
- `QS02`
- `WART01`
- `WART02`
- `WART03`
- `WART04`

#### Beispiel

Ein Demand verlangt einen Artikel, dessen Arbeitsplan `HE01` nutzt, aber kein Equipment kann `HE01`.

```json
{
  "snapshot": {
    "demands": [
      {
        "demandId": "D1",
        "articleId": "A1"
      }
    ],
    "articles": [
      {
        "articleId": "A1",
        "workPlanId": "WP1"
      }
    ],
    "workPlans": [
      {
        "workPlanId": "WP1",
        "manufacturing": {
          "workItemKey": "HE01"
        }
      }
    ],
    "equipment": [
      {
        "equipmentId": "E1",
        "equipmentKey": "EQ1",
        "workItems": ["ABF01"]
      }
    ]
  }
}
```

Meldung:

```text
Missing equipment for the following work item keys: HE01
```

---

### 2.11 Equipment benötigt Qualifikation, aber keine Worker-Qualifications vorhanden

Wenn Equipment eine `qualification` verlangt, aber keine `workerQualifications` vorhanden sind.

#### Beispiel

```json
{
  "snapshot": {
    "equipment": [
      {
        "equipmentId": "E1",
        "equipmentKey": "EQ1",
        "qualification": "Staplerschein"
      }
    ],
    "workerQualifications": []
  }
}
```

Meldung:

```text
Equipment requires qualifications but no worker qualifications defined. Note: Only qualification categories [...] are considered.
```

---

### 2.12 Fehlende Worker-Qualifikation für Equipment

Alle Equipment-Qualifikationen müssen bei mindestens einem Worker vorhanden sein. Es werden nur konfigurierte Qualifikationskategorien berücksichtigt.

#### Beispiel

```json
{
  "snapshot": {
    "equipment": [
      {
        "equipmentId": "E1",
        "equipmentKey": "EQ1",
        "qualification": "Spezialmaschine"
      }
    ],
    "workerQualifications": [
      {
        "worker": {
          "workerId": "W1",
          "workerName": "Max"
        },
        "qualifications": [
          {
            "qualification": "Staplerschein",
            "category": "A"
          }
        ]
      }
    ]
  }
}
```

Meldung:

```text
Missing worker qualifications required by equipment: Spezialmaschine. Note: Only qualification categories [...] are considered.
```

---

### 2.13 Equipment-Predecessor referenziert nicht existierendes Equipment

Alle Einträge in `equipment[].predecessors[]` müssen auf existierende `equipmentKey`s zeigen.

#### Beispiel

```json
{
  "snapshot": {
    "equipment": [
      {
        "equipmentId": "E1",
        "equipmentKey": "EQ1",
        "predecessors": ["EQ_DOES_NOT_EXIST"]
      }
    ]
  }
}
```

Meldung:

```text
Invalid equipment predecessor references: Equipment EQ1 references non-existent predecessor: EQ_DOES_NOT_EXIST
```

---

### 2.14 Packaging-Equipment-Compatibility referenziert nicht existierendes Equipment

Alle `packagingEquipmentCompatibility[].predecessors[]` müssen auf existierende `equipmentKey`s zeigen.

#### Beispiel

```json
{
  "snapshot": {
    "equipment": [
      {
        "equipmentId": "E1",
        "equipmentKey": "EQ1"
      }
    ],
    "packagingEquipmentCompatibility": [
      {
        "packaging": "P1",
        "predecessors": ["EQ_DOES_NOT_EXIST"]
      }
    ]
  }
}
```

Meldung:

```text
Invalid packaging equipment compatibility predecessor references: Packaging P1 references non-existent equipment predecessor: EQ_DOES_NOT_EXIST
```

---

### 2.15 Packaging ohne Vorgänger

Jeder Eintrag in `packagingEquipmentCompatibility` muss mindestens einen nicht-leeren `predecessor` haben.

#### Beispiel

```json
{
  "snapshot": {
    "packagingEquipmentCompatibility": [
      {
        "packaging": "P1",
        "predecessors": []
      }
    ]
  }
}
```

Meldung:

```text
Packaging without predecessors found: P1
```

---

### 2.16 Demand referenziert nicht existierendes Packaging

Wenn `demands[].packaging` gesetzt ist, muss es in `packagingEquipmentCompatibility[].packaging` existieren.

#### Beispiel

```json
{
  "snapshot": {
    "demands": [
      {
        "demandId": "D1",
        "articleId": "A1",
        "packaging": "P_DOES_NOT_EXIST"
      }
    ],
    "packagingEquipmentCompatibility": [
      {
        "packaging": "P1",
        "predecessors": ["EQ1"]
      }
    ]
  }
}
```

Meldung:

```text
Demand D1 references non-existent packaging: P_DOES_NOT_EXIST
```

---

### 2.17 Artikel referenziert nicht existierendes Standard-Packaging

Wenn `articles[].standardPackaging` gesetzt ist, muss es in `packagingEquipmentCompatibility[].packaging` existieren.

#### Beispiel

```json
{
  "snapshot": {
    "articles": [
      {
        "articleId": "A1",
        "standardPackaging": "P_DOES_NOT_EXIST"
      }
    ],
    "packagingEquipmentCompatibility": [
      {
        "packaging": "P1",
        "predecessors": ["EQ1"]
      }
    ]
  }
}
```

Meldung:

```text
Article A1 references non-existent packaging: P_DOES_NOT_EXIST
```

---

### 2.18 Human Availability fehlt bei vollständiger Solver-Validierung

Diese Prüfung ist in der vollständigen Snapshot-Validierung enthalten, aber **nicht** in der Message-Collection für `/validate-snapshot`.

Wenn Worker-Verfügbarkeit fehlt und ein Planungsmodus verwendet wird, der Human Availability benötigt, entsteht:

```text
Human availability data (workerAvailability) is missing or empty. Worker availability is required for shift-aligned planning period calculation. Either provide worker availability data or use 'demand_driven' planning start mode.
```

Beispiel:

```json
{
  "snapshot": {
    "workerAvailability": []
  }
}
```

---

## 3. Warnings, keine Business-Fehler

Diese Meldungen sind wichtig, führen aber alleine nicht zu HTTP 260.

### 3.1 Keine Worker-Qualifikationen und keine Worker-Verfügbarkeit

```text
No worker qualifications and availability data defined
```

Beispiel:

```json
{
  "snapshot": {
    "workerQualifications": null,
    "workerAvailability": null
  }
}
```

---

### 3.2 Keine Worker-Qualifikationen

```text
No worker qualifications defined
```

Beispiel:

```json
{
  "snapshot": {
    "workerQualifications": null,
    "workerAvailability": [
      {
        "worker": {
          "workerId": "W1"
        }
      }
    ]
  }
}
```

---

### 3.3 Keine Worker-Verfügbarkeit

```text
No worker availability defined
```

Beispiel:

```json
{
  "snapshot": {
    "workerQualifications": [
      {
        "worker": {
          "workerId": "W1"
        },
        "qualifications": []
      }
    ],
    "workerAvailability": null
  }
}
```

---

Workers with availability but no qualifications: W1
```

        "worker": {
          "workerId": "W1"
        }
      }
    ],
    "workerQualifications": []
  }
}
```

---

### 3.13 Isoliertes Equipment

Equipment sollte grundsätzlich in der Vorgänger-/Nachfolger-Kette verbunden sein. Ausnahmen gibt es für reine Start-, End- oder ausgeschlossene Work-Items.

```text
Isolated equipment detected: Equipment EQ1 is isolated: missing predecessor and missing successor
```

Beispiel:

```json
{
  "snapshot": {
    "equipment": [
      {
        "equipmentId": "E1",
        "equipmentKey": "EQ1",
        "workItems": ["HE01", "ABF01"],
        "predecessors": []
      }
    ]
  }
}
```

---

```text
No packaging equipment compatibility data found
```

Beispiel:

```json

    "packagingEquipmentCompatibility": null
  }
}
```

## 4. Vollständige Liste der Business-Error-Meldungsmuster

Hier die lückenlose Liste der fachlichen Error-Muster:

| Nr. | Fehler |
|---:|---|
| 1 | `{Item Type} IDs must be unique. Duplicates found: ...` |
| 2 | `{Item Type} IDs must not be empty. Empty IDs found: ...` |
| 3 | `Missing work plans for the following IDs: ...` |
| 4 | `Missing articles for the following demand article IDs: ...` |
| 5 | `Article {articleId} has invalid rel_density_min: ... Must be greater than 0.` |
| 6 | `Articles missing required department (Disponent2): ...` |
| 7 | `Departments (Disponent2) referenced by articles were not found: ...` |
| 8 | `Article {articleId} is missing work_item_configs for: ... Cannot process article.` |
| 9 | `Article {articleId} is missing work_item_config for HE01. Cannot process article.` |
| 10 | `Article {articleId} is missing work_item_config for ABF01. Cannot process article.` |
| 11 | `Article {articleId} has HE01 work_item_config with net_time_factor=..., ramp_up_time=... At least one ... must be > 0.` |
| 12 | `Article {articleId} has ABF01 work_item_config with net_time_factor=..., ramp_up_time=... At least one ... must be > 0.` |
| 13 | `Missing equipment for the following work item keys: ...` |
| 14 | `Equipment requires qualifications but no worker qualifications defined. Note: Only qualification categories [...] are considered.` |
| 15 | `Missing worker qualifications required by equipment: ... Note: Only qualification categories [...] are considered.` |
| 16 | `Invalid equipment predecessor references: Equipment ... references non-existent predecessor: ...` |
| 17 | `Invalid packaging equipment compatibility predecessor references: Packaging ... references non-existent equipment predecessor: ...` |
| 18 | `Packaging without predecessors found: ...` |
| 19 | `Demand {demandId} references non-existent packaging: ...` |
| 20 | `Article {articleId} references non-existent packaging: ...` |
| 21 | `Human availability data (workerAvailability) is missing or empty...` *(vollständige `validate_snapshot`-Prüfung, nicht im `/validate-snapshot` Message-Collector)* |

---

## 5. Rückgabeformat

Bei Business-Fehlern kommt typischerweise:

```json
{
  "validationMessages": [
    {
      "level": "error",
      "message": "Missing articles for the following demand article IDs: A_DOES_NOT_EXIST",
      "context": "validate_demand_article_ids"
    }
  ]
}
```

HTTP-Status:

- **200**: keine Error-Messages nach Verbosity-Filter
- **260**: mindestens eine `error`-Message
- **422**: Schema-/Request-Validierungsfehler
- **500**: unerwarteter interner Fehler

from typing import TYPE_CHECKING, Any, cast

from microservice_engine.adapters.validators.message_collector import collect_messages
from microservice_engine.adapters.validators.snapshot_validator import (
    validate_article_department_presence,
    validate_article_equipment_department_consistency,
    validate_demand_article_ids,
    validate_density_values,
    validate_equipment_connectivity,
    validate_equipment_department_presence,
    validate_equipment_predecessor_references,
    validate_equipment_unavailability_consistency,
    validate_equipment_worker_qualification_compatibility,
    validate_packaging_equipment_compatibility_references,
    validate_packaging_references,

    validate_work_plan_ids,

from microservice_engine.models.snapshot import Snapshot
from microservice_engine.models.validation_message import ValidationMessage

if TYPE_CHECKING:
    from microservice_engine.core.models import CollectedMessage

LEVEL_HIERARCHY = {
    "debug": 0,
    "info": 1,
    "warning": 2,
    "error": 3,
}


def filter_messages_by_verbosity(
    messages: list[ValidationMessage], verbosity: str
) -> list[ValidationMessage]:
    """
    Filter messages based on hierarchical verbosity level.

    Parameters
    ----------
    messages : list[ValidationMessage]
        List of validation messages to filter.
    verbosity : str
        Verbosity level for filtering messages.

    Returns
    -------
    list[ValidationMessage]
        Filtered list of messages at or above the given verbosity level.
    """
    min_level = LEVEL_HIERARCHY[verbosity]
    return [msg for msg in messages if LEVEL_HIERARCHY[msg.level] >= min_level]


def determine_status_code(messages: list[ValidationMessage]) -> int:
    """
    Determine HTTP status code based on highest message severity.

    Parameters
    ----------
    messages : list[ValidationMessage]
        List of validation messages.

    Returns
    -------
    int
        260 if any error-level message is present, else 200.
    """
    levels = {msg.level for msg in messages}
    if "error" in levels:
        return 260
    return 200


def collect_validation_messages(
    snapshot: Snapshot, verbosity: str
) -> tuple[list[ValidationMessage], int]:
    """
    Collect all validation messages from snapshot validation functions.

    Parameters
    ----------
    snapshot : Snapshot
        The snapshot to validate.
    verbosity : str
        Verbosity level for filtering messages.

    Returns
    -------
    tuple[list[ValidationMessage], int]
        Tuple of (filtered_messages, http_status_code)
    """
    # List of (function, args) in the order of validate_snapshot()
    validation_functions = [
        (validate_unique_ids, (snapshot.demands, "demand_id", "Demand")),
        (validate_unique_ids, (snapshot.articles, "article_id", "Article")),
        (validate_unique_ids, (snapshot.work_plans, "work_plan_id", "Work Plan")),
        (validate_unique_ids, (snapshot.equipment, "equipment_id", "Equipment")),
        (
            validate_unique_ids,
            (
                [wa.worker for wa in (snapshot.worker_availability or [])],
                "worker_id",
                "Worker Availability",
            ),
        ),
        (
            validate_unique_ids,
            (
                [wq.worker for wq in (snapshot.worker_qualifications or [])],
                "worker_id",
                "Worker Qualification",
            ),
        ),
        (validate_work_plan_ids, (snapshot,)),
        (validate_demand_article_ids, (snapshot,)),
        (validate_density_values, (snapshot,)),
        (validate_article_department_presence, (snapshot,)),
        (validate_equipment_department_presence, (snapshot,)),
        (validate_article_equipment_department_consistency, (snapshot,)),
        (validate_work_item_configs_completeness, (snapshot,)),
        (validate_start_end_operation_existence, (snapshot,)),
        (validate_worker_consistency, (snapshot,)),
        (validate_equipment_unavailability_consistency, (snapshot,)),
        (validate_work_item_equipment_availability, (snapshot,)),
        (validate_equipment_worker_qualification_compatibility, (snapshot,)),
        (validate_equipment_predecessor_references, (snapshot,)),
        (validate_packaging_equipment_compatibility_references, (snapshot,)),
        (validate_equipment_connectivity, (snapshot,)),
        (validate_packaging_references, (snapshot,)),
    ]
    all_collected: list[CollectedMessage] = []
    for func, args in validation_functions:
        decorated = collect_messages(cast(Any, func))
        all_collected.extend(decorated(*args))
    # Convert to ValidationMessage
    messages = [
        cast(ValidationMessage, msg.to_validation_message()) for msg in all_collected
    ]
    filtered = filter_messages_by_verbosity(messages, verbosity)
    status_code = determine_status_code(filtered)
    return filtered, status_code

              functionName: MCP tool /get_file_text_by_path
