              content: """Module contains utility functions for input snapshot validation."""

import contextlib
import logging
from collections.abc import Callable, Iterable
from datetime import datetime
from typing import Any, cast

from deepdiff.diff import DeepDiff

from microservice_engine.core.settings import settings
from microservice_engine.exceptions import (
    SnapshotValidationError,
)
from microservice_engine.models.equipment import Equipment
from microservice_engine.models.snapshot import Snapshot

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)


def _format_compact_id_list(
    values: Iterable[Any],
    max_items: int = 1000,
    *,
    separator: str = ", ",
    sort_key: Callable[[str], Any] | None = str,
    aggregate_duplicates: bool = False,
) -> str:
    """Format ID sets for logs with bounded output size."""
    string_values = [str(value) for value in values]
    sorted_values = sorted(string_values, key=sort_key) if sort_key is not None else string_values

    if aggregate_duplicates:
        aggregated_counts: dict[str, int] = {}
        aggregated_values: list[str] = []
        for value in sorted_values:
            if value not in aggregated_counts:
                aggregated_counts[value] = 1
                aggregated_values.append(value)
            else:
                aggregated_counts[value] += 1

        rendered_values = [
            f"{value} [{aggregated_counts[value]}x]" if aggregated_counts[value] > 1 else value
            for value in aggregated_values
        ]
    else:
        rendered_values = sorted_values

    if len(rendered_values) <= max_items:
        return separator.join(rendered_values)

    shown_values = separator.join(rendered_values[:max_items])
    omitted_count = len(rendered_values) - max_items
    return f"{shown_values} (+{omitted_count} more)"


def _normalize_department(value: str | None) -> str | None:
    """Normalize a single department value and treat blanks as missing."""
    if value is None:
        return None
    normalized = value.strip()
    return normalized if normalized else None


def _normalize_departments(values: list[str] | None) -> set[str]:
    """Normalize a department list and return unique non-empty values."""
    if not values:
        return set()
    return {normalized for value in values if (normalized := _normalize_department(value)) is not None}


def _format_equipment_label(equipment: Equipment) -> str:
    """Format equipment for human-readable validation messages."""
    return f"{equipment.equipment_key} ({equipment.equipment_id})"


def _format_worker_label(worker_id: str, worker_name: str | None) -> str:
    """Format worker details for human-readable validation messages."""
    normalized_worker_name = worker_name.strip() if worker_name is not None else ""
    if normalized_worker_name:
        return f"{worker_id} ({normalized_worker_name})"
    return worker_id


def _format_article_label(article_id: str, article_name: str | None) -> str:
    """Format article details for human-readable validation messages."""
    normalized_article_name = article_name.strip() if article_name is not None else ""
    if normalized_article_name:
        return f"{article_id} ({normalized_article_name})"
    return article_id


def validate_unique_ids(items: list[Any], id_field: str, item_type: str) -> None:
    """
    Validate that all IDs in the given list of items are unique and not empty.

    Parameters
    ----------
    items : List[Any]
        The list of items to be validated.
    id_field : str
        The field name of the ID in the items.
    item_type : str
        The type of items being validated (for error messages).

    Raises
    ------
    SnapshotValidationError
        If any IDs are not unique or are empty.
    """
    logger.debug("Validating unique IDs for %s", item_type)
    ids = [getattr(item, id_field) for item in items]
    duplicates = {item_id for item_id in ids if ids.count(item_id) > 1}
    empty_ids = {item_id for item_id in ids if not item_id}
    if duplicates:
        error_message = f"{item_type} IDs must be unique. Duplicates found: {', '.join(map(str, duplicates))}."
        logger.error(error_message)
        raise SnapshotValidationError(error_message)
    if empty_ids:
        error_message = f"{item_type} IDs must not be empty. Empty IDs found: {', '.join(map(str, empty_ids))}."
        logger.error(error_message)
        raise SnapshotValidationError(error_message)
    logger.debug("Unique ID validation passed for %s", item_type)


def validate_work_plan_ids(snapshot: Snapshot) -> None:
    """
    Validate that every work_plan_id in an article has a corresponding work_plan.

    Parameters
    ----------
    snapshot : Snapshot
        The snapshot data to be validated.

    Raises
    ------
    SnapshotValidationError
        If any work_plan_id in an article does not have a corresponding work_plan.
    """
    logger.debug("Validating work plan IDs in snapshot")
    article_work_plan_ids = {article.work_plan_id for article in snapshot.articles} if snapshot.articles is not None else set()
    work_plan_ids = {work_plan.work_plan_id for work_plan in snapshot.work_plans} if snapshot.work_plans is not None else set()

    missing_work_plan_ids = article_work_plan_ids - work_plan_ids
    if missing_work_plan_ids:
        error_message = (
            f"Missing work plans for the following IDs: {', '.join(str(work_plan_id) for work_plan_id in missing_work_plan_ids)}"
        )
        logger.error(error_message)
        raise SnapshotValidationError(error_message)
    logger.debug("Work plan ID validation passed")


def validate_demand_article_ids(snapshot: Snapshot) -> None:
    """
    Validate that every demand has a corresponding article based on the article ID.

    Parameters
    ----------
    snapshot : Snapshot
        The snapshot data to be validated.

    Raises
    ------
    SnapshotValidationError
        If any demand does not have a corresponding article.
    """
    logger.debug("Validating demand article IDs in snapshot")
    article_ids = {article.article_id for article in snapshot.articles} if snapshot.articles is not None else set()
    demand_article_ids = {demand.article_id for demand in snapshot.demands} if snapshot.demands is not None else set()

    missing_article_ids = demand_article_ids - article_ids
    if missing_article_ids:
        error_message = (
            f"Missing articles for the following demand article IDs: {', '.join(str(article_id) for article_id in missing_article_ids)}"
        )
        logger.error(error_message)
        raise SnapshotValidationError(error_message)
    logger.debug("Demand article ID validation passed")


def validate_worker_consistency(snapshot: Snapshot) -> None:
    """
    Validate that worker IDs are consistent in qualifications-availability.

    Log warnings for:
    1. Workers with availability but no qualifications
    2. Workers with qualifications but no availability
    3. Missing worker qualifications or availability data

    Parameters
    ----------
    snapshot : Snapshot
        The snapshot data to be validated.
    """
    logger.debug("Validating worker consistency in snapshot")

    # Handle missing qualification or availability data
    if snapshot.worker_qualifications is None and snapshot.worker_availability is None:
        logger.warning("No worker qualifications and availability data defined")
        return

    if snapshot.worker_qualifications is None:
        logger.warning("No worker qualifications defined")
        return

    if snapshot.worker_availability is None:
        logger.warning("No worker availability defined")
        return

    qualification_worker_ids: set[str] = set()
    qualification_worker_labels: dict[str, str] = {}
    availability_worker_ids: set[str] = set()

    # Collect worker IDs from worker_qualifications
    for qual in snapshot.worker_qualifications:
        if qual.worker and qual.worker.worker_id:
            worker_id = qual.worker.worker_id
            qualification_worker_ids.add(worker_id)
            qualification_worker_labels[worker_id] = _format_worker_label(worker_id, qual.worker.worker_name)

    # Collect worker IDs from worker_availability
    for avail in snapshot.worker_availability:
        if avail.worker and avail.worker.worker_id:
            availability_worker_ids.add(avail.worker.worker_id)

    # Log warnings for workers with availability but no qualifications
    workers_without_qualifications = availability_worker_ids - qualification_worker_ids
    if workers_without_qualifications:
        logger.warning(
            "Workers with availability but no qualifications: %s",
            ", ".join(sorted(workers_without_qualifications)),
        )

    # Log warnings for workers with qualifications but no availability
    workers_without_availability = qualification_worker_ids - availability_worker_ids
    if workers_without_availability:
        workers_without_availability_labels = [
            qualification_worker_labels.get(worker_id, worker_id) for worker_id in workers_without_availability
        ]
        logger.warning(
            "Workers with qualifications but no availability: %s",
            _format_compact_id_list(workers_without_availability_labels),
        )

    logger.debug("Worker consistency validation complete")


def validate_equipment_unavailability_consistency(snapshot: Snapshot) -> None:
    """
    Validate that equipment unavailability data is consistent.

    Log warnings for:
    1. Equipment unavailabilities referencing non-existent equipment IDs.
    2. Equipment entries without any unavailabilities provided.
    3. Missing equipment or missing equipment_unavailabilities section.

    Parameters
    ----------
    snapshot : Snapshot
        The snapshot data to be validated.
    """
    logger.debug("Validating equipment unavailability consistency")

    # Handle missing equipment or unavailabilities
    if snapshot.equipment is None or not snapshot.equipment:
        logger.warning("No equipment defined in snapshot")
        return

    if snapshot.equipment_unavailabilities is None or not snapshot.equipment_unavailabilities:
        logger.warning("Equipment defined but no unavailabilities provided")
        return

    # Set of equipment IDs from equipment list
    equipment_ids = {equip.equipment_id for equip in snapshot.equipment}

    # Set of equipment IDs from unavailabilities
    unavailability_equipment_ids = {unavail.equipment_id for unavail in snapshot.equipment_unavailabilities}

    # Check for unavailabilities for non-existent equipment
    non_existent_equipment = unavailability_equipment_ids - equipment_ids
    if non_existent_equipment:
        logger.warning(
            "Unavailability defined for non-existent equipment IDs: %s",
            _format_compact_id_list(non_existent_equipment),
        )

    # Check for equipment without unavailabilities
    missing_unavailability = equipment_ids - unavailability_equipment_ids
    if missing_unavailability:
        missing_unavailability_labels = [
            _format_equipment_label(equipment)
            for equipment in snapshot.equipment
            if equipment.equipment_id in missing_unavailability
        ]
        logger.warning(
            "Equipment without defined unavailabilities: %s",
            _format_compact_id_list(missing_unavailability_labels),
        )

    logger.debug("Equipment unavailability consistency validation complete")


def _extract_required_articles(snapshot: Snapshot) -> list[Any]:
    """Extract articles required by demands."""
    demand_article_ids = {demand.article_id for demand in snapshot.demands} if snapshot.demands is not None else set()
    return [article for article in (snapshot.articles or []) if article.article_id in demand_article_ids]


def _extract_required_work_plans(snapshot: Snapshot, required_articles: list[Any]) -> list[Any]:
    """Extract work plans required by the given articles."""
    required_work_plan_ids = {article.work_plan_id for article in required_articles}
    return [work_plan for work_plan in (snapshot.work_plans or []) if work_plan.work_plan_id in required_work_plan_ids]


def _extract_required_work_item_keys(required_work_plans: list[Any]) -> set[str]:  # noqa: C901, PLR0912
    """Extract all work item keys from the required work plans."""
    required_work_item_keys = set()
    for work_plan in required_work_plans:
        # Extract work item keys from all process steps
        if hasattr(work_plan, "preparation_aroma") and work_plan.preparation_aroma:
            required_work_item_keys.add(work_plan.preparation_aroma.work_item_key)
        if hasattr(work_plan, "preparation_powder") and work_plan.preparation_powder:
            required_work_item_keys.add(work_plan.preparation_powder.work_item_key)
        if hasattr(work_plan, "waiting_time1") and work_plan.waiting_time1:
            required_work_item_keys.add(work_plan.waiting_time1.work_item_key)
        if hasattr(work_plan, "manufacturing") and work_plan.manufacturing:
            required_work_item_keys.add(work_plan.manufacturing.work_item_key)
        if hasattr(work_plan, "resting_filtering1") and work_plan.resting_filtering1:
            required_work_item_keys.add(work_plan.resting_filtering1.work_item_key)
        if hasattr(work_plan, "quality_check1") and work_plan.quality_check1:
            required_work_item_keys.add(work_plan.quality_check1.work_item_key)
        if hasattr(work_plan, "waiting_time2") and work_plan.waiting_time2:
            required_work_item_keys.add(work_plan.waiting_time2.work_item_key)
        if hasattr(work_plan, "processing") and work_plan.processing:
            required_work_item_keys.add(work_plan.processing.work_item_key)
        if hasattr(work_plan, "resting_filtering2") and work_plan.resting_filtering2:
            required_work_item_keys.add(work_plan.resting_filtering2.work_item_key)
        if hasattr(work_plan, "quality_check2") and work_plan.quality_check2:
            required_work_item_keys.add(work_plan.quality_check2.work_item_key)
        if hasattr(work_plan, "waiting_time3") and work_plan.waiting_time3:
            required_work_item_keys.add(work_plan.waiting_time3.work_item_key)
        if hasattr(work_plan, "filling") and work_plan.filling:
            required_work_item_keys.add(work_plan.filling.work_item_key)
        if hasattr(work_plan, "waiting_time4") and work_plan.waiting_time4:
            required_work_item_keys.add(work_plan.waiting_time4.work_item_key)
    return required_work_item_keys


def _build_equipment_by_work_item(snapshot: Snapshot) -> dict[str, list[Any]]:
    """Build a lookup dictionary mapping work item keys to equipment."""
    equipment_by_work_item: dict[str, list[Any]] = {}
    if snapshot.equipment is not None:
        for equipment in snapshot.equipment:
            if equipment.work_items:
                for work_item_key in equipment.work_items:
                    if work_item_key not in equipment_by_work_item:
                        equipment_by_work_item[work_item_key] = []
                    equipment_by_work_item[work_item_key].append(equipment)
    return equipment_by_work_item


def _log_equipment_info(equipment_by_work_item: dict[str, list[Any]]) -> None:
    """Log equipment count and details per work item."""
    logger.debug(
        "Equipment count per work item: %s",
        ", ".join(f"{key}: {len(eq_list)}" for key, eq_list in equipment_by_work_item.items()),
    )
    logger.debug(
        "Equipment per work item: %s",
        {key: [equip.equipment_key for equip in eq_list] for key, eq_list in equipment_by_work_item.items()},
    )


def validate_work_item_equipment_availability(snapshot: Snapshot) -> None:
    """
    Validate that every work item has at least one compatible equipment entry.

    Parameters
    ----------
    snapshot : Snapshot
        The snapshot data to be validated.

    Raises
    ------
    SnapshotValidationError
        If any required work item key does not have a compatible equipment.
    """
    logger.debug("Validating equipment availability for required work items")

    # Skip validation if collections are empty
    if snapshot.demands is None or not snapshot.demands:
        logger.debug("No demands defined, skipping work item equipment validation")
        return

    if snapshot.articles is None or not snapshot.articles:
        logger.debug("No articles defined, skipping work item equipment validation")
        return

    if snapshot.work_plans is None or not snapshot.work_plans:
        logger.debug("No work plans defined, skipping work item equipment validation")
        return

    if snapshot.equipment is None or not snapshot.equipment:
        logger.debug("No equipment defined, skipping work item equipment validation")
        return

    required_articles = _extract_required_articles(snapshot)
    required_work_plans = _extract_required_work_plans(snapshot, required_articles)
    required_work_item_keys = _extract_required_work_item_keys(required_work_plans)
    equipment_by_work_item = _build_equipment_by_work_item(snapshot)
    _log_equipment_info(equipment_by_work_item)

    available_keys = set(equipment_by_work_item.keys())
    exempt_work_item_keys: list[str] = [
        "QS01",
        "QS02",
        "WART01",
        "WART02",
        "WART03",
        "WART04",
    ]
    missing_keys = required_work_item_keys - available_keys - set(exempt_work_item_keys)

    if missing_keys:
        error_message = "Missing equipment for the following work item keys: " + ", ".join(sorted(missing_keys))
        logger.error(error_message)
        raise SnapshotValidationError(error_message)

    logger.debug("Equipment availability validation passed")


def validate_density_values(snapshot: Snapshot) -> None:
    """
    Validate that all articles have rel_density_min > 0.

    Raises SnapshotValidationError if not.
    """
    logger.debug("Validating article density values (rel_density_min > 0)")
    articles = snapshot.articles or []
    for article in articles:
        rel_density_min = getattr(article, "rel_density_min", None)
        if rel_density_min is None or rel_density_min <= 0:
            msg = f"Article {getattr(article, 'article_id', '?')} has invalid rel_density_min: {rel_density_min}. Must be greater than 0."
            logger.error(msg)
            raise SnapshotValidationError(msg)
    logger.debug("Article density validation passed")


def validate_article_department_presence(snapshot: Snapshot) -> None:
    """Validate that each article has a non-empty department_id (Disponent2/BM0110)."""
    logger.debug("Validating article department presence")
    if snapshot.articles is None or not snapshot.articles:
        logger.debug("No articles defined in snapshot")
        return

    missing_department_articles = [
        _format_article_label(article.article_id, article.article_name)
        for article in snapshot.articles
        if _normalize_department(article.department_id) is None
    ]
    if missing_department_articles:
        msg = f"Articles missing required department (Disponent2): {_format_compact_id_list(missing_department_articles)}"
        logger.error(msg)
        raise SnapshotValidationError(msg)

    logger.debug("Article department presence validation passed")


def validate_equipment_department_presence(snapshot: Snapshot) -> None:
    """Warn if equipment has no valid departments (Disponent2/BM0110) configured."""
    logger.debug("Validating equipment department presence")
    if snapshot.equipment is None or not snapshot.equipment:
        logger.debug("No equipment defined in snapshot")
        return

    equipment_without_departments = [
        _format_equipment_label(equipment)
        for equipment in snapshot.equipment
        if not _normalize_departments(equipment.departments)
    ]

    if equipment_without_departments:
        logger.warning(
            "Equipment without departments (Disponent2): %s",
            _format_compact_id_list(equipment_without_departments),
        )

    logger.debug("Equipment department presence validation complete")


def validate_article_equipment_department_consistency(snapshot: Snapshot) -> None:
    """Validate article department coverage across configured equipment departments.

    This check is strict only when at least one equipment department is configured.
    If no equipment department data is configured at all, a warning is logged and
    validation continues for backward compatibility with legacy snapshots.
    """
    logger.debug("Validating article to equipment department consistency")
    if snapshot.articles is None or not snapshot.articles:
        logger.debug("No articles defined in snapshot")
        return

    article_departments = {
        normalized
        for article in snapshot.articles
        if (normalized := _normalize_department(article.department_id)) is not None
    }
    if not article_departments:
        logger.debug("No valid article departments to validate")
        return

    equipment_departments: set[str] = set()
    for equipment in snapshot.equipment or []:
        equipment_departments.update(_normalize_departments(equipment.departments))

    if not equipment_departments:
        logger.warning(
            "No equipment departments configured; skipping article to equipment "
            "department consistency validation",
        )
        return

    missing_departments = article_departments - equipment_departments
    if missing_departments:
        msg = f"Departments (Disponent2) referenced by articles were not found: {_format_compact_id_list(missing_departments)}"
        logger.error(msg)
        raise SnapshotValidationError(msg)

    logger.debug("Article to equipment department consistency validation passed")


def validate_work_item_configs_completeness(snapshot: Snapshot) -> None:
    """
    Validate that all required work item keys from the work plan's ProcessStep.

    Attributes are present in the article's work_item_configs.
    Raises SnapshotValidationError if any required config is missing.
    """
    logger.debug("Validating completeness of work_item_configs for all articles")
    if not snapshot.articles or not snapshot.work_plans:
        logger.debug("No articles or work plans to validate work_item_configs completeness")
        return
    # Build a cache: work_plan_id -> set of required work_item_keys
    workplan_key_cache: dict[str, set[str]] = {}
    process_step_fields = [
        "preparation_aroma",
        "preparation_powder",
        "waiting_time1",
        "manufacturing",
        "resting_filtering1",
        "quality_check1",
        "waiting_time2",
        "processing",
        "resting_filtering2",
        "quality_check2",
        "waiting_time3",
        "filling",
        "waiting_time4",
    ]
    workplan_map = {wp.work_plan_id: wp for wp in snapshot.work_plans}
    for article in snapshot.articles:
        wp_id = getattr(article, "work_plan_id", None)
        if not wp_id or wp_id not in workplan_map:
            continue  # work_plan_id validation is handled elsewhere
        if wp_id not in workplan_key_cache:
            wp = workplan_map[wp_id]
            keys = set()
            for field in process_step_fields:
                step = getattr(wp, field, None)
                if step is not None and hasattr(step, "work_item_key"):
                    key = getattr(step, "work_item_key", None)
                    if key:
                        keys.add(key)
            workplan_key_cache[wp_id] = keys
        required_keys = workplan_key_cache[wp_id]
        present_keys = {wic.work_item_key for wic in getattr(article, "work_item_configs", [])}
        missing = required_keys - present_keys
        if missing:
            msg = (
                f"Article {getattr(article, 'article_id', '?')} is missing work_item_configs for: "
                f"{', '.join(sorted(missing))}. Cannot process article."
            )
            logger.error(msg)
            raise SnapshotValidationError(msg)
    logger.debug("WorkItemConfigs completeness validation passed")


def validate_start_end_operation_existence(snapshot: Snapshot) -> None:
    """
    Validate that every article has both HE01 and ABF01 work item configs and for each.

    At least one of net_time_factor or ramp_up_time is > 0.
    Raises SnapshotValidationError if missing or invalid.
    """
    logger.debug("Validating existence and timing of HE01 and ABF01 in work_item_configs")
    if not snapshot.articles:
        logger.debug("No articles to validate start/end operation existence")
        return
    for article in snapshot.articles:
        configs = getattr(article, "work_item_configs", [])
        keys = {wic.work_item_key: wic for wic in configs}
        for key in ("HE01", "ABF01"):
            if key not in keys:
                msg = f"Article {getattr(article, 'article_id', '?')} is missing work_item_config for {key}. Cannot process article."
                logger.error(msg)
                raise SnapshotValidationError(msg)
            net_time_factor = getattr(keys[key], "net_time_factor", None)
            ramp_up_time = getattr(keys[key], "ramp_up_time", None)
            if (net_time_factor is None or net_time_factor <= 0) and (ramp_up_time is None or ramp_up_time <= 0):
                msg = (
                    f"Article {getattr(article, 'article_id', '?')} has {key} work_item_config with "
                    f"net_time_factor={net_time_factor}, ramp_up_time={ramp_up_time}. "
                    f"At least one of net_time_factor or ramp_up_time must be > 0. "
                    f"This prevents a valid processing chain."
                )
                logger.error(msg)
                raise SnapshotValidationError(msg)
    logger.debug("Start/end operation existence and timing validation passed")


def _validate_human_availability_exists(snapshot: Snapshot) -> None:
    """
    Validate that human availability data exists in the snapshot.

    This validation ensures that worker availability information is present,
    which is required for shift-aligned planning period start calculation.

    Parameters
    ----------
    snapshot : Snapshot
        The snapshot data to be validated.

    Raises
    ------
    SnapshotValidationError
        If worker availability data is missing or empty.
    """
    logger.debug("Validating human availability exists")
    if snapshot.worker_availability is None or len(snapshot.worker_availability) == 0:
        msg = (
            "Human availability data (workerAvailability) is missing or empty. "
            "Worker availability is required for shift-aligned planning period calculation. "
            "Either provide worker availability data or use 'demand_driven' planning start mode."
        )
        logger.error(msg)
        raise SnapshotValidationError(msg)
    logger.debug("Human availability validation passed")


def validate_snapshot(snapshot: Snapshot) -> None:
    """
    Validate the snapshot data.

    Validates:
    - Uniqueness of IDs
    - Existence of work plans
    - Worker consistency
    - Equipment unavailability consistency
    - Work item equipment availability
    - Equipment worker qualification compatibility
    - Equipment predecessor references
    - Packaging equipment compatibility references
    - Equipment connectivity

    Parameters
    ----------
    snapshot : Snapshot
        The snapshot data to be validated.

    Raises
    ------
    SnapshotValidationError
        If any validation checks fail that are considered errors.
    """
    logger.info("Starting snapshot validation")
    if snapshot.demands is not None:
        validate_unique_ids(snapshot.demands, "demand_id", "Demand")
    if snapshot.articles is not None:
        validate_unique_ids(snapshot.articles, "article_id", "Article")
    if snapshot.work_plans is not None:
        validate_unique_ids(snapshot.work_plans, "work_plan_id", "Work Plan")
    if snapshot.equipment is not None:
        validate_unique_ids(snapshot.equipment, "equipment_id", "Equipment")
    if snapshot.worker_availability is not None:
        validate_unique_ids(
            [wa.worker for wa in snapshot.worker_availability],
            "worker_id",
            "Worker Availability",
        )
    if snapshot.worker_qualifications is not None:
        validate_unique_ids(
            [wq.worker for wq in snapshot.worker_qualifications],
            "worker_id",
            "Worker Qualification",
        )
    validate_work_plan_ids(snapshot)
    validate_demand_article_ids(snapshot)
    validate_density_values(snapshot)
    validate_article_department_presence(snapshot)
    validate_equipment_department_presence(snapshot)
    validate_article_equipment_department_consistency(snapshot)
    validate_work_item_configs_completeness(snapshot)
    validate_start_end_operation_existence(snapshot)
    validate_worker_consistency(snapshot)
    validate_equipment_unavailability_consistency(snapshot)
    validate_work_item_equipment_availability(snapshot)
    validate_equipment_worker_qualification_compatibility(snapshot)
    validate_equipment_predecessor_references(snapshot)
    validate_packaging_equipment_compatibility_references(snapshot)
    validate_equipment_connectivity(snapshot)
    validate_packaging_references(snapshot)
    _validate_human_availability_exists(snapshot)
    logger.debug("Snapshot validation completed successfully")


def convert_datetime_strings(data: dict[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """Convert datetime strings in the dictionary to datetime objects.

    Parameters
    ----------
    data : dict
        The dictionary containing data with datetime strings.

    Returns
    -------
    dict
        The dictionary with datetime strings converted to datetime objects.
    """
    for key, value in data.items():
        if isinstance(value, str):
            with contextlib.suppress(ValueError):
                converted_value = datetime.fromisoformat(value.replace("Z", "+00:00"))
                if converted_value != value:
                    logger.debug(
                        "Converted datetime string for key '%s': %s -> %s",
                        key,
                        value,
                        converted_value,
                    )
                data[key] = converted_value
        elif isinstance(value, dict):
            data[key] = convert_datetime_strings(value)
        elif isinstance(value, list):
            data[key] = [convert_datetime_strings(item) if isinstance(item, dict) else item for item in value]
    return data


def validate_snapshot_integrity(original_snapshot_data: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
    """Validate the integrity of the snapshot data."""
    logger.debug("Validating snapshot integrity")

    # Validate and create a Snapshot instance
    snapshot = Snapshot.model_validate(original_snapshot_data)

    # Convert the validated snapshot back to a dictionary
    validated_snapshot_data = snapshot.model_dump(by_alias=True, mode="json")

    # Pre-process dictionaries to make them comparable
    # This removes None values from validated data that weren't in original data
    validated_snapshot_data = _clean_none_values(validated_snapshot_data, original_snapshot_data)

    # Convert datetime strings to datetime objects for comparison
    original_snapshot_data = convert_datetime_strings(original_snapshot_data)
    validated_snapshot_data = convert_datetime_strings(validated_snapshot_data)

    # Compare the original and validated snapshot data
    differences = DeepDiff(original_snapshot_data, validated_snapshot_data, ignore_order=True)

    if differences:
        error_message = (
            f"Snapshot data integrity check failed. Original and validated snapshot data do not match. Differences: {differences}."
        )
        logger.error(error_message)
        logger.debug("Differences: %s", differences)
        raise SnapshotValidationError(error_message)

    logger.debug("Snapshot integrity validation passed")


def _clean_none_values(validated_data: dict[str, Any], original_data: dict[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """
    Remove None values from validated data that weren't present in the original data.

    This function addresses the specific issue with Pydantic models (like Equipment)
    where optional fields (such as wasteVolume) are initialized with None even when
    they are missing in the original JSON data, causing comparison inconsistencies.

    Parameters
    ----------
    validated_data : dict
        The validated dictionary created from model_dump() of a Pydantic model,
        potentially containing None values for optional fields.
    original_data : dict
        The original dictionary for comparison, representing the raw input data.

    Returns
    -------
    dict
        The cleaned validated dictionary with None values removed for keys
        that didn't exist in the original data.

    Examples
    --------
    >>> validated = {"name": "Mischtopf", "waste_volume": None}
    >>> original = {"name": "Mischtopf"}
    >>> _clean_none_values(validated, original)
    {'name': 'Mischtopf'}
    """
    # Create a new dictionary to store the cleaned data
    result: dict[str, list[Any] | dict[str, Any] | Any] = {}

    # Process each key-value pair in the validated data
    for key, value in validated_data.items():
        # Skip None values that weren't in original data
        if value is None and key not in original_data:
            continue

        # Process nested dictionaries recursively
        if isinstance(value, dict) and key in original_data and isinstance(original_data[key], dict):
            result[key] = _clean_none_values(value, original_data[key])

        # Process lists with potential nested structures
        elif isinstance(value, list) and key in original_data and isinstance(original_data[key], list):
            cleaned_list = _clean_list_values(value, original_data[key])
            result[key] = cleaned_list

        # Include all other values as they are
        else:
            result[key] = value

    return result


def _clean_list_values(validated_list: list[Any], original_list: list[Any]) -> list[Any]:
    """
    Process lists from validated data, handling potential nested dictionaries.

    This is a helper function for _clean_none_values that specifically handles lists,
    which may contain nested dictionaries that need recursive cleaning.

    Parameters
    ----------
    validated_list : list
        The list from validated data, potentially containing dictionaries with None
        values.
    original_list : list
        The corresponding list from original data for comparison.

    Returns
    -------
    list
        The processed list with None values handled in any nested dictionaries.
    """
    # Simple case: empty lists or non-dictionary elements
    if not validated_list or not any(isinstance(item, dict) for item in validated_list):
        return validated_list

    # Lists containing dictionaries need element-wise processing
    result_list = []

    # For lists of dictionaries, try to match elements by position
    for i, val_item in enumerate(validated_list):
        if isinstance(val_item, dict):
            # Use empty dict as fallback only if the item in original_list is not a dict
            if i < len(original_list) and isinstance(original_list[i], dict):
                orig_item = original_list[i]
                result_list.append(_clean_none_values(val_item, orig_item))
            else:
                result_list.append(val_item)
        else:
            # Non-dictionary items are kept as is
            result_list.append(val_item)

    return result_list


def _collect_equipment_qualifications(
    equipment_list: list[Any] | None,
) -> tuple[set[str], dict[str, int]]:
    """
    Collect qualification requirements from equipment.

    Parameters
    ----------
    equipment_list : list[Any] | None
        List of equipment items or None

    Returns
    -------
    tuple[set[str], dict[str, int]]
        A tuple containing:
        - Set of unique qualification requirements
        - Dictionary mapping qualifications to their usage count
    """
    required_qualifications: set[str] = set()
    required_qual_counts: dict[str, int] = {}

    if not equipment_list:
        return required_qualifications, required_qual_counts

    for equipment in equipment_list:
        if equipment.qualification and equipment.qualification.strip():
            qualification = equipment.qualification
            required_qualifications.add(qualification)
            required_qual_counts[qualification] = required_qual_counts.get(qualification, 0) + 1

    return required_qualifications, required_qual_counts


def _collect_worker_qualifications(
    worker_qualifications: list[Any] | None,
) -> tuple[set[str], dict[str, int]]:
    """
    Collect qualifications from workers.

    Only qualifications with categories listed in ALLOWED_WORKER_QUALIFICATION_CATEGORIES
    setting are included, matching the filtering behavior in resource_initializer.py.

    Parameters
    ----------
    worker_qualifications : list[Any] | None
        List of worker qualification items or None

    Returns
    -------
    tuple[set[str], dict[str, int]]
        A tuple containing:
        - Set of unique worker qualifications
        - Dictionary mapping qualifications to their count
    """
    available_qualifications: set[str] = set()
    available_qual_counts: dict[str, int] = {}

    if not worker_qualifications:
        return available_qualifications, available_qual_counts

    # Get allowed categories from settings (same as resource_initializer)
    allowed_categories: list[str] = cast(list[str], settings.ALLOWED_WORKER_QUALIFICATION_CATEGORIES)

    for worker_qual in worker_qualifications:
        if worker_qual.qualifications:
            for qual in worker_qual.qualifications:
                if qual and qual.qualification and qual.qualification.strip() and qual.category in allowed_categories:
                    # Apply category filtering
                    qualification = qual.qualification
                    available_qualifications.add(qualification)
                    available_qual_counts[qualification] = available_qual_counts.get(qualification, 0) + 1

    return available_qualifications, available_qual_counts


def _validate_qualification_compatibility(
    required_qualifications: set[str],
    available_qualifications: set[str],
    required_qual_counts: dict[str, int],
    available_qual_counts: dict[str, int],
) -> None:
    """
    Compare qualification sets and validate compatibility.

    Parameters
    ----------
    required_qualifications : set[str]
        Set of qualifications required by equipment
    available_qualifications : set[str]
        Set of qualifications provided by workers
    required_qual_counts : dict[str, int]
        Count of each required qualification
    available_qual_counts : dict[str, int]
        Count of each available qualification

    Raises
    ------
    SnapshotValidationError
        If any required qualification is not available from workers
    """
    # Log required qualifications statistics
    if required_qual_counts:
        logger.debug(
            "Required qualifications usage: %s",
            ", ".join(f"{qual}: {count}" for qual, count in required_qual_counts.items()),
        )

    # Log available qualifications statistics
    if available_qual_counts:
        logger.debug(
            "Available qualifications by workers: %s",
            ", ".join(f"{qual}: {count}" for qual, count in available_qual_counts.items()),
        )

    # Warn about qualifications with critically low worker counts
    low_worker_qualifications = {qual: count for qual, count in available_qual_counts.items() if count < 3}  # noqa: PLR2004
    if low_worker_qualifications:
        logger.warning(
            "Qualifications with fewer than 3 workers: %s",
            ", ".join(f"{qual}: {count}" for qual, count in sorted(low_worker_qualifications.items())),
        )

    # Compare qualification sets
    missing_qualifications = required_qualifications - available_qualifications
    unused_qualifications = available_qualifications - required_qualifications

    # Handle missing qualifications
    if missing_qualifications:
        allowed_categories = cast(list[str], settings.ALLOWED_WORKER_QUALIFICATION_CATEGORIES)
        error_message = (
            f"Missing worker qualifications required by equipment: {', '.join(sorted(missing_qualifications))}. "
            f"Note: Only qualification categories {allowed_categories} are considered."
        )
        logger.error(error_message)
        raise SnapshotValidationError(error_message)

    # Log unused qualifications
    if unused_qualifications:
        logger.debug(
            "Worker qualifications not required by any equipment: %s",
            ", ".join(sorted(unused_qualifications)),
        )


def validate_equipment_worker_qualification_compatibility(snapshot: Snapshot) -> None:
    """
    Validate that all equipment qualification reqs have matching worker qualifications.

    Parameters
    ----------
    snapshot : Snapshot
        The snapshot data to be validated.

    Raises
    ------
    SnapshotValidationError
        If any equipment qualification does not have a matching worker qualification.
    """
    logger.debug("Validating equipment and worker qualification compatibility")

    # Extract equipment qualifications
    if snapshot.equipment is None or not snapshot.equipment:
        logger.debug("No equipment defined in snapshot")
        return

    # Collect required qualifications from equipment
    required_qualifications, required_qual_counts = _collect_equipment_qualifications(snapshot.equipment)

    # Extract worker qualifications
    if snapshot.worker_qualifications is None or not snapshot.worker_qualifications:
        if required_qualifications:
            allowed_categories = cast(list[str], settings.ALLOWED_WORKER_QUALIFICATION_CATEGORIES)
            error_message = (
                f"Equipment requires qualifications but no worker qualifications defined. "
                f"Note: Only qualification categories {allowed_categories} are considered."
            )
            logger.error(error_message)
            raise SnapshotValidationError(error_message)
        logger.debug("No worker qualifications defined in snapshot")
        return

    # Collect available qualifications from workers
    available_qualifications, available_qual_counts = _collect_worker_qualifications(snapshot.worker_qualifications)

    # Validate compatibility between required and available qualifications
    _validate_qualification_compatibility(
        required_qualifications,
        available_qualifications,
        required_qual_counts,
        available_qual_counts,
    )

    logger.debug("Equipment and worker qualification compatibility validation passed")


def validate_equipment_predecessor_references(snapshot: Snapshot) -> None:
    """
    Validate that all equipment predecessors reference existing equipment keys.

    Parameters
    ----------
    snapshot : Snapshot
        The snapshot data to be validated.

    Raises
    ------
    SnapshotValidationError
        If any equipment references a predecessor that doesn't exist.
    """
    logger.debug("Validating equipment predecessor references")

    # If equipment is None or empty, skip validation
    if snapshot.equipment is None or not snapshot.equipment:
        logger.debug("No equipment defined in snapshot")
        return

    # Create a set of all available equipment keys
    available_equipment_keys = {equip.equipment_key.strip() for equip in snapshot.equipment}
    logger.debug("Available equipment keys: %s", available_equipment_keys)

    # Check for invalid predecessor references
    invalid_references: list[dict[str, str]] = []

    for equip in snapshot.equipment:
        if equip.predecessors:
            invalid_references.extend(
                [
                    {
                        "equipment_key": equip.equipment_key,
                        "invalid_predecessor": pred,
                    }
                    for pred in equip.predecessors
                    if pred.strip() not in available_equipment_keys
                ]
            )

    # If invalid references found, raise an error
    if invalid_references:
        error_messages = []
        for ref in invalid_references:
            msg = "Equipment {} references non-existent predecessor: {}".format(ref["equipment_key"], ref["invalid_predecessor"])
            error_messages.append(msg)

        error_message = "Invalid equipment predecessor references: {}".format("; ".join(error_messages))
        logger.error(error_message)
        raise SnapshotValidationError(error_message)

    logger.debug("Equipment predecessor reference validation passed")


def validate_packaging_equipment_compatibility_references(snapshot: Snapshot) -> None:
    """
    Validate that all predecessors in packaging equipment have valid reference .

    Parameters
    ----------
    snapshot : Snapshot
        The snapshot data to be validated.

    Raises
    ------
    SnapshotValidationError
        If any packaging equipment compatibility references a predecessor that doesn't
        exist.
    """
    logger.debug("Validating packaging equipment compatibility predecessor references")

    # Skip validation if either equipment or packagingEquipmentCompatibility is missing
    if (
        snapshot.equipment is None
        or not snapshot.equipment
        or snapshot.packaging_equipment_compatibility is None
        or not snapshot.packaging_equipment_compatibility
    ):
        logger.debug("Skipping packaging equipment compatibility validation due to missing data")
        return

    # Create a set of all available equipment keys
    available_equipment_keys = {equip.equipment_key.strip() for equip in snapshot.equipment}
    logger.debug("Available equipment keys: %s", available_equipment_keys)

    # Check for invalid predecessor references
    invalid_references: list[dict[str, str]] = []

    for compatibility in snapshot.packaging_equipment_compatibility:
        if compatibility.predecessors:
            invalid_references.extend(
                [
                    {
                        "packaging": compatibility.packaging,
                        "invalid_predecessor": pred,
                    }
                    for pred in compatibility.predecessors
                    if pred.strip() not in available_equipment_keys
                ]
            )

    # If invalid references found, raise an error
    if invalid_references:
        error_messages = []
        for ref in invalid_references:
            msg = "Packaging {} references non-existent equipment predecessor: {}".format(ref["packaging"], ref["invalid_predecessor"])
            error_messages.append(msg)

        error_message = ("Invalid packaging equipment compatibility predecessor references: {}").format("; ".join(error_messages))
        logger.error(error_message)
        raise SnapshotValidationError(error_message)

    logger.debug("Packaging equipment compatibility predecessor reference validation passed")


def _build_equipment_successor_map(equipment_list: list[Any]) -> dict[str, list[str]]:
    """
    Build a mapping of equipment to their successors based on predecessor relationships.

    Parameters
    ----------
    equipment_list : list[Any]
        List of equipment items

    Returns
    -------
    dict[str, list[str]]
        Dictionary mapping equipment keys to their successor equipment keys
    """
    # Dictionary to store successors for each equipment
    successors: dict[str, list[str]] = {equip.equipment_key: [] for equip in equipment_list}

    # Process predecessors to build successor relationships
    for equip in equipment_list:
        if equip.predecessors:
            for pred in equip.predecessors:
                if pred in successors:
                    successors[pred].append(equip.equipment_key)

    return successors


def _identify_special_equipment(
    equipment_work_items: dict[str, set[str]],
) -> tuple[set[str], set[str], set[str]]:
    """
    Identify equipment that falls into special categories (start, end, excluded).

    Parameters
    ----------
    equipment_work_items : dict[str, set[str]]
        Dictionary mapping equipment keys to their work items

    Returns
    -------
    tuple[set[str], set[str], set[str]]
        Tuple containing:
        - Set of equipment keys that exclusively handle start work items
        - Set of equipment keys that exclusively handle end work items
        - Set of equipment keys that exclusively handle excluded work items
    """
    # Identify equipment that EXCLUSIVELY handles start work items
    exclusive_start_equipment = set()
    # Identify equipment that EXCLUSIVELY handles end work items
    exclusive_end_equipment = set()
    # Identify equipment that EXCLUSIVELY handles excluded work items
    exclusive_excluded_equipment = set()

    for equip_key, work_items in equipment_work_items.items():
        # Pre-cast settings lists to sets of strings for fast membership checks
        start_items = set(cast(list[str], settings.START_WORK_ITEMS))
        end_items = set(cast(list[str], settings.END_WORK_ITEMS))
        excluded_items = set(cast(list[str], settings.EXCLUDED_WORK_ITEMS))
        # Equipment is exclusive start equipment if ALL its work items are in START_WORK_ITEMS
        if work_items and all(item in start_items for item in work_items):
            exclusive_start_equipment.add(equip_key)
        # Equipment is exclusive end equipment if ALL its work items are in END_WORK_ITEMS
        if work_items and all(item in end_items for item in work_items):
            exclusive_end_equipment.add(equip_key)
        # Equipment is exclusive excluded equipment if ALL its work items are in EXCLUDED_WORK_ITEMS
        if work_items and all(item in excluded_items for item in work_items):
            exclusive_excluded_equipment.add(equip_key)

    return exclusive_start_equipment, exclusive_end_equipment, exclusive_excluded_equipment


def _check_equipment_isolation(
    equipment: Equipment,
    successors: dict[str, list[str]],
    exclusive_start_equipment: set[str],
    exclusive_end_equipment: set[str],
) -> dict[str, bool | str] | None:
    """
    Check if an equipment item is isolated in the production network.

    Parameters
    ----------
    equipment : Any
        Equipment item to check
    successors : dict[str, list[str]]
        Dictionary mapping equipment keys to their successor equipment keys
    exclusive_start_equipment : set[str]
        Set of equipment keys that exclusively handle start work items
    exclusive_end_equipment : set[str]
        Set of equipment keys that exclusively handle end work items

    Returns
    -------
    dict[str, bool | str] | None
        Dictionary with isolation details if isolated, otherwise None
    """
    has_predecessor = bool(equipment.predecessors)
    has_successor = bool(successors[equipment.equipment_key])

    # Check if equipment is properly connected, considering exceptions
    is_exclusive_start_equipment = equipment.equipment_key in exclusive_start_equipment
    is_exclusive_end_equipment = equipment.equipment_key in exclusive_end_equipment

    # Equipment should have predecessors unless it's EXCLUSIVELY for start work items
    valid_predecessor = has_predecessor or is_exclusive_start_equipment

    # Equipment should have successors unless it's EXCLUSIVELY for end work items
    valid_successor = has_successor or is_exclusive_end_equipment

    if not (valid_predecessor and valid_successor):
        return {
            "equipment_key": equipment.equipment_key,
            "missing_predecessor": not valid_predecessor,
            "missing_successor": not valid_successor,
        }
    return None


def validate_equipment_connectivity(snapshot: Snapshot) -> None:
    """
    Identify isolated equipment in the production network and log warnings.

    Equipment should have at least one predecessor and one successor, with exceptions
    for:
    - Equipment that is EXCLUSIVELY used for start work items (e.g., HE01) may have no
      predecessors
    - Equipment that is EXCLUSIVELY used for end work items (e.g., ABF01) may have no
      successors
    - Equipment that is EXCLUSIVELY used for excluded work items (e.g., VOAR01, VOPU01)
      is exempt from connectivity checks

    Parameters
    ----------
    snapshot : Snapshot
        The snapshot data to be validated.
    """
    logger.debug("Validating equipment connectivity")

    # If equipment is None or empty, skip validation
    if snapshot.equipment is None or not snapshot.equipment:
        logger.debug("No equipment defined in snapshot")
        return

    # Build successor relationships map
    successors = _build_equipment_successor_map(snapshot.equipment)

    # Create dictionaries mapping equipment keys to their work item capabilities
    equipment_work_items = {}
    for equip in snapshot.equipment:
        equipment_work_items[equip.equipment_key] = set(equip.work_items) if equip.work_items else set()

    # Identify special equipment categories
    exclusive_start_equipment, exclusive_end_equipment, exclusive_excluded_equipment = _identify_special_equipment(equipment_work_items)

    # Debug logging
    logger.debug(
        "Equipment that exclusively handles start work items: %s",
        exclusive_start_equipment,
    )
    logger.debug(
        "Equipment that exclusively handles end work items: %s",
        exclusive_end_equipment,
    )
    logger.debug(
        "Equipment that exclusively handles excluded work items: %s",
        exclusive_excluded_equipment,
    )
    logger.debug("Equipment successor map: %s", successors)

    # Validate equipment connectivity
    isolated_equipment = []

    for equip in snapshot.equipment:
        # Skip equipment that exclusively handles excluded work items
        if equip.equipment_key in exclusive_excluded_equipment:
            continue

        isolation_info = _check_equipment_isolation(equip, successors, exclusive_start_equipment, exclusive_end_equipment)
        if isolation_info:
            isolated_equipment.append(isolation_info)

    # If isolated equipment found, log a warning
    if isolated_equipment:
        error_messages = []
        for item in isolated_equipment:
            msg_prefix = "Equipment {} is isolated: ".format(item["equipment_key"])
            issues = []
            if item["missing_predecessor"]:
                issues.append("missing predecessor")
            if item["missing_successor"]:
                issues.append("missing successor")
            msg = msg_prefix + " and ".join(issues)
            error_messages.append(msg)

        warning_payload = _format_compact_id_list(
            error_messages,
            max_items=10,
            separator="; ",
            sort_key=None,
            aggregate_duplicates=True,
        )
        warning_message = f"Isolated equipment detected: {warning_payload}"
        logger.warning(warning_message)

    logger.debug("Equipment connectivity validation complete")


def validate_packaging_references(snapshot: Snapshot) -> None:
    """
    Validate that packaging IDs.

    Three validations are performed:
    - Check if all packaging IDs referenced in demands and articles exist
    - Ensure that all packaging IDs have at least one predecessor

    Parameters
    ----------
    snapshot : Snapshot
        The snapshot object to validate.

    Raises
    ------
    SnapshotValidationError
        If a demand or article references a non-existent packaging, or if a packaging
        has no predecessors.
    """
    logger.debug("Validating packaging references in snapshot")

    # Skip validation if packaging_equipment_compatibility is missing
    if snapshot.packaging_equipment_compatibility is None:
        logger.warning("No packaging equipment compatibility data found")
        return

    # Create a set of all available packaging IDs (strip whitespace)
    available_packaging_ids = {compatibility.packaging.strip() for compatibility in snapshot.packaging_equipment_compatibility}

    # Validate each packaging has at least one predecessor
    for compatibility in snapshot.packaging_equipment_compatibility:
        if not compatibility.predecessors or not any(pred.strip() for pred in compatibility.predecessors):
            error_message = f"Packaging without predecessors found: {compatibility.packaging}"
            logger.error(error_message)
            raise SnapshotValidationError(error_message)

    # Validate demand packaging references
    if snapshot.demands is not None:
        for demand in snapshot.demands:
            if demand.packaging and demand.packaging.strip() not in available_packaging_ids:
                error_message = f"Demand {demand.demand_id} references non-existent packaging: {demand.packaging}"
                logger.error(error_message)
                raise SnapshotValidationError(error_message)

    # Validate article standardPackaging references
    if snapshot.articles is not None:
        for article in snapshot.articles:
            if article.standard_packaging and article.standard_packaging.strip() not in available_packaging_ids:
                error_message = f"Article {article.article_id} references non-existent packaging: {article.standard_packaging}"
                logger.error(error_message)
                raise SnapshotValidationError(error_message)

    logger.debug("Packaging references validation passed")

              content: """Decorator and logging handler for collecting validation messages (SPEC_22)."""

import logging
from collections.abc import Callable
from functools import wraps

from microservice_engine.core.models import CollectedMessage
from microservice_engine.exceptions import SnapshotValidationError


class MessageCollectingHandler(logging.Handler):
    """Custom logging handler that collects messages during validation."""

    def __init__(self, function_name: str) -> None:
        super().__init__()
        self.function_name = function_name
        self.collected_messages: list[CollectedMessage] = []

    def emit(self, record: logging.LogRecord) -> None:
        """
        Emit a log record by converting it into a CollectedMessage and storing it.

        Parameters
        ----------
        record : logging.LogRecord
            The log record to be processed and collected.
        """
        message = CollectedMessage(
            level=record.levelname.lower(),
            raw_message=record.getMessage(),
            function_name=self.function_name,
        )
        self.append_if_new(message)

    def append_if_new(self, message: CollectedMessage) -> None:
        """Append message only if an identical entry does not already exist."""
        if message not in self.collected_messages:
            self.collected_messages.append(message)


def collect_messages(
    func: Callable[..., object],
) -> Callable[..., list[CollectedMessage]]:
    """
    Collect log messages and exceptions from validation functions.

    - Captures all logger.info(), logger.warning(), logger.error() calls
    - Converts SnapshotValidationError exceptions to error messages
    - Adds function name context to all messages
    - Returns messages instead of raising exceptions
    """

    @wraps(func)
    def wrapper(*args: object, **kwargs: object) -> list[CollectedMessage]:
        logger = logging.getLogger(func.__module__)
        handler = MessageCollectingHandler(func.__name__)
        logger.addHandler(handler)
        try:
            func(*args, **kwargs)
        except SnapshotValidationError as e:
            handler.append_if_new(
                CollectedMessage(level="error", raw_message=str(e), function_name=func.__name__)
            )
        finally:
            logger.removeHandler(handler)
        return handler.collected_messages

    return wrapper
